# ACCESS MSK Track Clinical Exclusion Diagnoses - CMS ACCESS Model API v0.9.11

## ValueSet: ACCESS MSK Track Clinical Exclusion Diagnoses 

 
This value set contains ICD-10-CM diagnosis codes that represent clinical exclusions that would prevent a patient from participating in the ACCESS Model Musculoskeletal (MSK) track. 

 **References** 

This value set is not used here; it may be used elsewhere (e.g. specifications and/or implementations that use this content)
